shared_focus = {
	id = WW1_WO_FRA_war_council
	icon = GFX_focus_BRA_reach_out_to_military_dem
	relative_position_id = FR3_diplomatic_effort
	x = 25 y = 0 cost = 3

	allow_branch = { has_focus_tree = french_focus_ww1 }
	available = {
		has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1
		has_completed_focus = FR3_diplomatic_effort
	}

	cancel_if_invalid = yes
	available_if_capitulated = yes
	ai_will_do = { factor = 4 modifier = { factor = 2 has_war = yes } }
	search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }
	completion_reward = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = WW1_WO_FRA_war_council_programme_tt
		effect_tooltip = {
			set_country_flag = WW1_WO_programme_open
			add_stability = 0.02
			army_experience = 10
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = { limit = { has_start_date < 1936.1.1 date < 1936.1.1 NOT = { has_country_flag = WW1_WO_FRA_war_council_rewarded } }
				set_country_flag = WW1_WO_FRA_war_council_rewarded
				set_country_flag = WW1_WO_programme_open
				add_stability = 0.02
				army_experience = 10
			}
		}
	}
	# Scoped WW1 native layout: thematic groups and compact chains.
